公司簡介 
中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅 ， 早期業
務專注於傳統砂輪 ， 後取得鑽石碟技術專利
授權，並因應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，
切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，
砂輪事業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。
2Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻
記、KTC、MKS、MGT) 25%、DBU 31%、
SBU 43%。

主要客戶：台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導
體、英特爾、欣興 
主要競爭對手：

王彥鈞 stanley.wang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

中砂 (1560 TT) 
今天的我，會超越你想像的極限

永豐觀點 
早晚得千金。 
 投資評價與建議 
目標價調升至 860 元：(1) 2Q26 因產品組合改善，毛利率提早達到 40%且下半年
繼續墊高，(2) 半導體大趨勢受惠股，除半導體/記憶體客戶持續擴產外，包含電晶
體結構改變、晶背供電、先進封裝均可帶動中砂營運成長。目標價隨2028 年 EPS
上修至 860 元(33x 2028 EPSF)。

ESG 評析 
中砂企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向平
均得分分別為 A 等與 A 等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2025 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用

### Page 2

2

中砂為先進製程鑽石碟第一把交椅：中砂成立於 1953 年，2005 年上市，目前資
本額約 14.6 億元，早期業務專注於傳統砂輪，後取得鑽石碟技術專利授權，並因
應晶圓代工廠客戶在地化策略，切入半導體領域。中砂主要有三大事業部，砂輪事
業部、鑽石事業部、晶圓事業部。2Q26 營收組成為 ABU(含砂輪子公司如鴻記、
KTC、MKS、MGT) 25%、DBU 31%、SBU 43%。中砂總部位於新北市鶯歌區，
砂輪、鑽石相關產品主要在鶯歌、樹林、湖口廠生產，而再生 /測試晶圓主要在新
竹竹北、竹南廠生產，另在日本有 1 座廠房、泰國有 2 座廠房。客戶涵蓋半導體/
記憶體製造商如台積電、美光、聯電、意法半導體、英特爾，以及載板廠欣興等。 
2Q26 優於研究部預期：中砂 2Q26 營收 24.9 億(+8.7%QoQ，+17.9%YoY)，所
有事業部需求均回升，包含(1) ABU 部分，AC 砂輪(傳統砂輪)受惠傳產機械、工具
機需求回升，而 BD 砂輪(鑽石/氮化硼砂輪)則隨 ABF 客戶需求增加而成長，營收
季增 16.3%，(2) DBU 受惠公司主要客戶在 2/1.6nm 製程出貨增加，營收季增
9.1%，(3) SBU 在產能提前建置後維持滿載，加上特殊晶圓出貨增加，營收季增
4.4%。毛利率因產能利用率提升、在客戶高階製程市占率較高而提升至 40.1%，
業外 0.6 億主要來自投資利益約 0.44 億，帶動稅後淨利達 5.2 億(+19.9%QoQ，
+96.5%YoY)，EPS 3.49 元。獲利表現優於預期，主因ABU、SBU 產品組合改善。 
獲利率成長具延續性：我們認為中砂後續獲利可穩步改善，主要來產品組合改善、
產能利用率提升，以及費用下降，包含(1) ABU 中高毛利的 BD 砂輪營收比重已由
1/3 提升至 50%，隨載板等高階應用需求增加，將持續推升 ABU 獲利率，公司亦
計劃增加 CVD 鑽石鍍膜所需設備因應。(2) SBU 新增產能以高階測試晶圓為主，
目前產能預期滿載至年底，2027 年則透過去瓶頸和增加高階設備持續擴產，有利
產品組合改善。(3) DBU 隨產能規模擴大，以及積極推動生產自動化，在人力增幅
低於產能增幅下，營運槓桿將逐步顯現。(4) 公司自 3Q24 起提列單季約 2,700 萬
的員工認股權費用，當時預期攤提約 10 季度，相關影響預期在 2H26-1H27 陸續
結束，亦有助於費用率下降。

### Page 3

3

受惠半導體大趨勢，中砂迎來結構性成長：除客戶擴產帶動出貨量增加以外，我們
認為製程複雜度提升也將持續增加中砂單位晶圓/封裝的產品用量，使公司具備優
於 WFE 市場的結構性成長機會，主要來自：(1) 先進製程放量：主要客戶 2/3nm
產能在 2026-2028 年持續擴張，公司鑽石碟市占率維持約 70-80%，直接受惠先
進製程投片增加；A14 預期 2028 年量產，目前研發階段已開始貢獻營收，隨A14
家族後續放量，營收貢獻可望顯著擴大；(2) 晶背供電提高 CMP 密度：隨部分先
進製程導入晶背供電，新增背面製程步驟將提高 CMP 製程道數，帶動單位晶圓鑽
石碟耗用量 ；(3) 高階測試/特殊晶圓需求增加： 邏輯與記憶體先進製程擴產將同步
推升高階測試晶圓需求 ， 使SBU 成長動能由傳統再生晶圓進一步延伸至高階應用 ；
(4) 先進封裝與 PLP：Foundry／IDM 持續擴充先進封裝產能將推升 BD 砂輪需
求，而 2028-2029 年面板級封裝逐步導入量產後，研磨面積放大亦有望進一步提
升單位產品用量。 
上調未來三年獲利預估 ：研究部預估中砂 2026 年全年營收 103.4 億(+27.0%YoY)，
毛利率 39.5%，稅後淨利 21.5 億(+58.5%YoY)，EPS 14.47 元(原估 13.65 元)。
2027 年營收 129.3 億 (+25.0%YoY) ，毛利率 41.5% ，稅後淨利 30.1 億
(+39.9%YoY)，EPS 20.26 元(原估 18.70 元)。2028 年營收 147.2 億(+13.9%YoY)，
毛利率 43.9%，稅後淨利 38.7 億(+28.6%YoY)，EPS 26.05 元(原估 22.46 元)。 
圖一：SBU 產能擴充規劃

Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT-US)  
財報 FIRST TAKE — AI 產能大基建的最大贏家，財測超標但「不夠驚喜」

資料來源：Factset，Bloomberg 
 評論及分析 (Overall Summary) 
Applied Materials, Inc. (AMAT) 2026 財年第三季 (Q3 FY2026) 財報展現公司
受惠於全球 AI 基礎設施的快速建設與對先進晶片需求激增的強勁動能。憑藉
在 AI 運算相關高效能與高價值技術領域的領導地位，公司實現創紀錄的單季
營收與獲利，並創下歷史最高的季增長率。在半導體系統與全球服務業務  
(AGS) 雙引擎發力推動下，單季營收達到 91 億美元。儘管面臨建廠無塵室空
間受限與短期擴產的人力成本挑戰，但客戶為確保供應鏈穩定，積極提供長
達八個季度的滾動預測，針對技術藍圖的能見度甚至延伸至 2030 年。隨著業
界迅速朝向先進邏輯製程、 DRAM 及先進封裝  (Advanced Packaging) 發
展，Applied Materials 透過其深厚的材料工程技術與 EPIC 創新平台，已進一
步鞏固其市場領導地位。展望 2026 財年第四季，在 DRAM 強勁復甦與領先
晶圓代工邏輯製程推動下，公司預期營收將再次大幅跳升，持續作為 AI 發展
趨勢下的主要受惠者。 
公司財報重點數據： 
Applied Materials 在 2026 財年第三季展現強勁的財務表現，單季總營收達 
91 億美元，不僅季增 15%，年增更達 25%，創下歷史新高。受惠於價值定價 
(Value-based pric ing) 策略及高利潤產品組合， Non-GAAP 毛利率微升至  
50.4%，較上季成長  40 個基點，較去年同期增加  150 個基點；同時  Non-
GAAP 營業利益率也擴張至 34% 的歷史新高，年增 330 個基點。亮眼的營運
表現帶動第三季 Non-GAAP 每股盈餘 (EPS) 達 3.50 美元（+41%YoY）。 
公司業績表現： 
⚫ 半導體系統 (Semiconductor Systems) 業務：營收達創紀錄的 70 億美元 
(+27%YoY)。其中，DRAM 營收 (包含 HBM 封裝) 較去年同期增長 52% 
創下新高。在沉積 (包含 PVD、CVD 及磊晶)、材料改質、材料移除 (蝕刻
與 CMP) 以及製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 等多個領域皆創下營收紀錄。該部門 
Non-GAAP 毛利率升至 55.4%，Non-GAAP 營業利益達 27 億美元。 
⚫ 全球服務 (Applied Global Services, AGS) 業務：營收達 18 億美元 (年增 
22%)。受惠於訂閱服務成長與高交易性零件需求，並利用  AI 與倉儲自動
化提升營運效率，部門毛利率達 35.6%，營業利益率達 30.1%。目前全球
已有超過 37,000 個機台連接至其專屬的 AIx 軟體平台。 
⚫ 其他業務 (Other)：營收約 2.94 億美元，主要為顯示器 (Display) 業務。

複委託報告 August 14, 2026

### Page 2

2
公司展望與產業前景：
⚫ 2026 財政年度第四季指引 ：預估營收將落在  102.5 億美元區間  (+
51%YoY)。半導體系統營收預計約 79 億美元 (+62%YoY)，AGS 營收約
18.4 億美元  (年增  22%)。Non-GAAP 毛利率預估約  50.4%，Non-
GAAP EPS 預計為 4.02 美元 (+85%YoY)。
⚫ 先進封裝與 PDC 前景：預期 2026 日曆年的整體先進封裝營收將成長超
過 70%，而製程診斷與控制 (PDC) 業務在今年也將成長超過 50%。
⚫ 產能擴張與中國市場 ：為了應對強勁的市場需求，公司本季新增超過
1,500 名製造與客服人員，並計畫在 2028 年前將系統產出能力翻倍。中
國市場本季佔半導體系統與 AGS 總營收的 26%，在 28 奈米代工邏輯投
資推動下，預期今年將持續成長。
電話會議 Q&A 重點摘要：
⚫ 訂單能見度與長期合約 ：管理層指出，針對先進晶片供不應求的情況，
大型客戶提供了長達八個季度的詳細滾動式預測，部分關鍵客戶針對技

術藍圖的能見度更延伸至  2030 年。這種高能見度顯著改善了公司與供
應鏈的產能規劃。
⚫ 毛利率與定價策略動態 ：分析師關注毛利率在營收大幅增長下於下一季
度的指引為何持平。管理層解釋，短期內因大量招募工程師與服務人員
產生了「爬坡成本」(Ramp costs) 抵銷了部分規模經濟優勢。然而，透
過「價值定價」(Value-based pricing) 策略，半導體系統毛利率已穩居
55% 以上，長期來看整體毛利率仍將保持增長趨勢。
⚫ ICAPS 與各終端市場復甦：針對 ICAPS ( 物聯網、通訊、汽車、電源與
感測器) 市場，公司預期該領域的設備消化期已接近尾聲，隨著客戶端
產能利用率上升以及電源、光子晶片的亮點需求，預期 ICAPS 整體在今
年及明年皆將重返成長軌道。
公司簡介：
Applied Materials, Inc. 是全球領先的半導體與顯示器設備及材料工程解決方
案供應商。該公司致力於推動資料中心、人工智慧  (AI) 運算基礎架構與先進
應用的底層晶片製造技術。其廣泛的產品線涵蓋沉積、材料移除、先進封裝

至製程診斷控制系統 (PDC)，並透過 Applied Global Services (AGS) 提供高
度依賴  AI 預 測 分 析 的 機 台 最 佳 化 解 決 方 案 。 近 期 更 透 過 設 立 EPIC
(Equipment and Process Innovation and Commercialization) 中心，進一
步深化與頂尖晶片製造商及研究機構的早期共同創新 (Co-innovation)。
競爭對手與公司自身價值：
Applied Materials 在全球半導體製造設備市場中面臨眾多同業的競爭。相較
於競爭對手， AMAT 的核心價值與護城河在於其最廣泛且高度互補的技術組
合 (特別在先進邏輯製程、DRAM 及先進封裝)。該公司透過全新的 EPIC 平台
讓客戶在架構發展的極早期階段便能接觸最新設備創新，從而加速新技術導
入並提升晶圓廠的生產良率與單位面積產出。這種深度的技術整合與協助客
戶提升廠區投資報酬率的能力，使其在 AI 軍備競賽中擁有無可取代的產業地

位。

營運展望 
1. 2Q26 伺服器導軌帶動營收獲利 再創歷史新高：營收表現強勁，且毛利率與營
利率續創新高水位，即便有匯兌損失與未分配盈餘費用，稅後淨利動能仍非常
強勁，單季獲利超過七個股本 。川湖第二季營收 108 億元、+98.7% QoQ 、
+156.1% YoY， 毛利率 87.4%。 營業利益 89 億元 、+143.3% QoQ、+199.7% YoY，
營業利益率 82.1%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 71 億元，每股盈餘 74.38 元。 
2. 日夜輪班產能最大化，下半年營運逐季成長，毛利與營利率可維持高檔：第一
季產能利用率約落於 60%，受惠於強勁拉貨動能，第二季產能利用率已大幅攀
升至 70 ~ 80% 區間。為滿足客戶急迫且龐大的訂單交期需求，公司針對瓶頸
站點積極加開日夜輪班以最大化實質產出。除原有的四大  CSP 客戶外，近期
更囊括多家規模龐大的新興 CSP 客戶，因此儘管具體訂單出貨需配合客戶的
動態時程，但整體需求強勁，公司明確看好下半年營運表現，預期下半年度營
收相較上半年 (Half-on-Half) 具備明確成長空間。 
3. 新產品荷重標準提高，川湖機構設計開模量產能力，有助維持競爭優勢：隨著 
AI 晶片與 CSP 的專案生命週期由過去的 1.5 ~ 2 .0 年大幅縮短至目前的 0.5 
~ 1.0 年甚至更短 ， 連帶使模具製造到滿足客戶需求的整合時間大幅壓縮 。 一旦
客戶確認規格，川湖最快僅需 1 ~ 2 個月即可正式進入量產階段，且在自動化
生產、模具開發與機構設計的可靠度上深獲全球  CSP 客戶高度信任。伺服器
導軌在 AI 伺服器的總成本佔比極低，但卻是影響機櫃穩定性與維運運作的核
心關鍵安全件，因此終端客戶更看重供應商的技術可靠度與交期，進而鞏固川
湖的議價能力與高毛利結構。 
4. 美國休士頓廠即將量產，高雄川益三期五期提早擴建，落成後產能將倍增：美
國休士頓廠已取得 ISO 國際認證 ， 且前期試量產皆已達標 ， 即將正式轉入量產
階段 ， 就近服務北美終端客戶 。在台灣高雄方面 ， 既有的伺服器導軌主力廠房 ，
川益一期與二期完工於 2022 年，總建坪為 11 萬平方米 。由於市場需求強勁 ，
後續的川益三期與五期擴建計畫提前 1.5 ~ 2.0 年至 2026 年底動工，總投資
金額達新台幣 100 億元，其總建坪高達 22 萬平方米 ，落成後伺服器導軌產能
將倍增，為公司備妥充裕產能。 
5. 隨著新產品組合優化川湖獲利將再創新高：近期各家雲端服務供應商的新基礎
架構陸續啟動，隨著客戶眾多各類型自製晶片伺服器規格升級，以及寬型機櫃
荷重標準提升 ， 川湖營運獲利將續創歷史新高 。 合庫預估 2026 年營收 570 億
元、+225.8% YoY，毛利率 84.8%。營業利益 456 億元、+277.1% YoY，營業
利益率 80.0%。歸屬母公司稅後淨利 370 億元，每股盈餘 388.33 元。 
6. ESG (川湖在 2026 年上市公司治理評鑑結果為 51 ~ 65%)：川湖從產品的設計
階段即考量「易回收、低污染、省資源、省能源」的概念，在生產的過程中嚴

營收走勢圖

研究員/莊佳蓉 EVA8894122@uni-psg,com

研究部對川湖維持買進投資建議，主係 : (1) 2Q26 營收與獲利大幅
優於預期，毛利率飆升至 87.4%，創歷史新高；(2) 7 月單月營收已
站穩 60 億元以上，管理層對下半年展望正向；(3) 美國廠下半年進
入量產，並持續投入三、五期廠房，面積為二期規模的一倍，未來產
能無虞；(4) 新平台單價大幅提升，川湖產業護城河持續強化。綜上
所述，研究部上修 2027 年營收至 824 億元(原估 346 億元)，年增
56.9%，EPS 上修至 534.41 元(原估 207.00 元)，給予 29 倍本益比，
目標價上修至 15,500 元。

重點摘要： 
1. 2Q26營收獲利同步噴出，毛利率飆升至87.4%創新高 :  
2Q26營收108億元(原估64億元)，季增98.7%，年增156.1%，較
原預期大幅超越68.4%，主因HGX B300、GB300與ASIC AI伺服
器需求強勁所致。毛利率87.4%(原估77.0%)，季增9.7pts，大
幅優於研究部預期，主係產品效率提升、經濟規模效益，營益
率82.1%(原估68.4%)，季增15.0pts。業外收支2.29億元，EPS
達74.38元(原估38.97元)，優於預期。。

2. 7月營收站穩60億元以上，3Q26同步大幅上修 
7月營收64億元，月增44%，年增356%，較2Q26單月均值(約
36億元)明顯跳升，管理層指引產能利用率已達70~80%。研究
部考量下半年VR新平台 、ASIC陸續放量 ， 加上休士頓新產能陸
續開出，研究部上修 3Q26營收至179.6億元(原估69.5億元)，
EPS上修至116.38元(原估40.27元)。

3. 新平台單價優於預期，2027年大幅上修 
根據供應鏈調查，川湖在新一代導軌單價(不論在VR、AWS T3、
AMD平台)，遠高於研究部先前預期，故目前已大幅上修上修
2027年營收至824億元(原估346億元)。市佔方面，由於平台迭
代快速，供應商需在NPI早期即參與開發，才能快速配合客戶
設計，故川湖具備先行者優勢，預估川湖在AI伺服器機櫃滑軌
(GPU、ASIC) 市佔率超過八~九成，HGX、客製化機櫃等，因進
入門檻更高，預估有望達獨佔地位，川湖持續居導軌產業龍頭
地位。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

4. 產能佈局到位，美國廠下半年投產 
產能方面，美國(休士頓)廠預計3Q26末投產，就近支援美系客
戶需求。此外，二期廠區面積約11萬平方公尺，預期再投入三
期、五期廠區，面積合計約22萬平方公尺(為原二期規模的一
倍)，預計今年底左右動工，投資金額預估約100億元，將導入
更多自動化產線，有助提升生產效率並強化市場競爭力。 。

5. 產業地位穩固，大幅上修獲利並維持買進 
綜上所述，研究部上修2026年營收至525億元(原估260億元)，
年增200%，EPS上修至348.50元(原估160.66元)。並同步上修
2027年營收至824億元(原估346億元)，年增56.9%，EPS上修至
534.41元(原估207.00元)。考量2Q財報大幅優於預期、下半年
產能持續開出，且護城河優勢持續強化，給予29倍本益比，目
標價上修至15,500元(2027 EPS*29 PER)，維持買進建議。

附錄 一 ： 評 等 之 標 準 
評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

順德(2351) 
Q2毛利率優預期，AI持續推升營運 
投資評等 目標價 
買進 $252 
前次投資建議：2026.07.16 
「逢低買進」 ，目標價: $240

報告日期：2026.08.10 研 究 員：邱岳霖

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ AI 導線架動能強勁，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元。 
➢ AI 產品全面放量，均熱片加入貢獻，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利再上層樓，EPS 8.4 元，
考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，上調投資評等為「買進」 ，目標價252 元(30X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間 48.2%。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/7 收盤價： 170.00 ➢ 展望 2026 年，預期AI 相關營收將倍數增長，車用受
惠庫存調整結束與新車款推出，均熱片預計認證後進度
可望加速。受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，
同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本業持續改善。綜上所
述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐 投
顧 上 修 順 德 2026 年營收  137.1 億 元 ，YoY 
+33.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 
億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。 
➢ 展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十kW 往100 kW 
以上，過往 48V/54V 供電架構已不敷使用， 預期在
+-400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動AI導線架營
收持續高速增長；此外順德近兩年積極耕耘的散熱新事
業，預計在2027年將會開花結果，目前順德的均熱片
可做到約 30μm 的薄型化水準，優於同業約 50–60
μm，具備一定競爭優勢，預期在通過認證後產能陸續
開出，同時隨著CPU/GPU更大尺寸擴張，可望成為新
一輪成長主軸；車用則在電動化與在地化趨勢下維持穩
健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動率維持高檔下，毛利率
可望續揚，兆豐投顧預估順德 2027 年營收 154.8 億
元，YoY +12.9%，毛利率 21.3%，年增 1ppt，稅後
淨利 16.3 億元，YoY +30%，EPS 8.4 元，營運再創
新高。考量到今年獲利上修且未來長期營運動能強勁，
上 調 投 資 評 等 為 「 買 進 」， 目 標 價252元 (30X 
27(F)PER)，潛在上漲空間48.2%。

個股報告 
資料來源：CMoney；兆豐投顧預估

營 運 分 析

順德 2025年跨入 AI server領域，2026年散熱領域正式發酵 
順德(2351)成立於 1967年，以辦公文具起家，1983 年跨入半導體導線架，
並於 2010 年與日本三菱伸銅(mitsubishi-shindoh)技術合作，取得導線架原
料銅合金製程設備及技術授權，目前產品涵蓋 IC 導線架、功率/分離式元件
導線架、LED 導線架、音圈馬達彈片，終端應用遍及車用、工業與消費性電
子。順德目前在全球導線架市佔排名前五，但在 Power IC 導線架則排名第
一。順德過往多以車用產品為主，不過隨著資料中心電源架構的改變，順德
與英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管理經驗移轉至資料中心，順德成為全台唯一 AI 
server 導線架主力供應商，並於 2025 年開始出高功率導線架與穩壓器。而
順德自 2024 年布局均熱片跨足散領域，並於 2026 年開始出 CPO Switch產
品，而多家 CSP 之 CPU與 GPU 也有望於本年度驗證後開始出貨。

- 3 - 
個股報告 
車用持續復甦與 AI 導線架需求強勁，26Q2 營收與毛利率皆優預期，惟費
用增，EPS 1.6 元 
順德(2351)公告 26Q2 財報：營收 35.5 億元，QoQ +26.4%/YoY +36.6%，，
優於預期，主要受惠車用與消費性需求同步回溫、AI 導線架需求高速增長，
全產品線均季增超過 20%；同時在報價調漲與稼動率提升、且生產效率提升
下，毛利率 20.8%，季增1.7ppt，創近年新高，也優於研究部預估之 18.5%，
不過因人事獎金與一次性 RBA 移工費用(約 5,000 萬元)提列，使費用季增
21%至 3.2 億元，故稅後淨利 3 億元，QoQ +36.8%/YoY 虧為盈，EPS 1.6
元，略低於前次上修後之 1.7 元。

車用展望佳、AI 放量加速，上修獲利預估，26Q3 EPS 1.8 元 
順德(2351)公告 7 月營收：營收 13.3 億元，MoM +9.4%/YoY +60.8% ，再
創單月新高，展望 26Q3，兆豐投顧上修營收至 36.5 億元，QoQ +3%/YoY 
+43.6%，主要動能來自車用需求全面復甦與  AI 導線架供不應求，車用方
面：除歐美車廠需求延續外，陸系新品也將推出，預期車用成長動能將延續，
此外 AI 導線於 Q2 有近 30%季增長，預期在新產能開出下，營收將逐月創高，
而在產品組合優化與稼動率提升下，預估毛利率維持 20%高檔水準，同步上
修稅後淨利 3.5 億元，QoQ +15.4% /YoY + 301%，EPS 1.8 元(原估 1.75
元)。

AI 導線架動能強勁，車市需求明顯回溫，均熱片新案持續發酵，2026 年將
重返榮耀，EPS 6.5 元 
展望 2026 年，順德最大成長動能來自 AI 產品線，隨著伺服器電源架構改
變，PSU 瓦數持續提升，高功率導線架需求將被徹底激發，從高壓電網、
SST(固態變壓器)、Power Stage、48V bus converter、800V HVDC 與 BBU 
皆須用到高功率導線架，而順德自 2024 年攜手英飛凌將 800V 車用電源管
理經驗移轉至資料中心 ，使順德成為全球  AI server 導線架主力供應商之
一，目前有超過 40 支產品，同時在擴產效益帶動下，預期 2026 年 AI 相關
營收將倍數增長；在車用方面，受惠庫存調整結束，同時電動車需求激增與
新車款推出，預期全年呈雙位數增長；在均熱片方面，公司耕耘近兩年，近
期通過 2 CSP 客戶廠房稽核，預計認證後進度可望加速，更大尺寸產品用於 
CPU/GPU 將陸續驗證出貨，惟現階段出貨量較小且產能有限。毛利率方面：
受惠 26H1 漲價帶動，稼動率維持高檔，同時產品組合轉佳下，將有助於本
業持續改善。綜上所述，考量 H1 財報優於預期，展望更趨正向，兆豐投顧
上修順德 2026 年營收 137.1 億元，YoY +3 3.8%，毛利率 20.3%，年增 
7ppt，稅後淨利 12.5 億元，YoY +313%，EPS 6.5 元。

- 4 - 
個股報告 
AI 產品全面放量，均熱片加入貢獻，車用穩健成長，2027 年獲利再上層樓，
EPS 8.4 元 
展望 2027 年，隨著單機櫃功耗自數十 kW 往 100 kW 以上，過往 48V/54V 
供電架構已不敷使用，故產業正加速朝 HVDC(高壓直流)架構演進，而順德
過去幾年與歐美大廠合作開發高功率、高散熱、高可靠度的導線架，將成為
主要受惠者，預期在+-400V/800V HVDC 架構滲透率提升帶動 AI 導線架營
收持續高速增長；此外順德近兩年積極耕耘的散熱新事業，預計在 2027 年
將會開花結果，目前順德的均熱片可做到約 30μm 的薄型化水準，優於同業
約 50–60μm，具備一定競爭優勢，預期在通過認證後產能陸續開出，同時
隨著 CPU/GPU 更大尺寸擴張，可望成為新一輪成長主軸；車用則在電動化
與在地化趨勢下維持穩健成長。在產品組合優化與稼動率維持高檔下，毛利
率可望續揚，兆豐投顧預估順德 2027 年營收 154.8 億元，YoY +12.9%，
毛利率 21.3%，年增 1ppt，稅後淨利 16.3 億元，YoY +30%，EPS 8.4 元，
營運再創新高。

- 1 - 
個股報告 
義隆(2458) 
NB缺料導致 26H2受影響 
投資評等 目標價 
區間操作 $150 
前次投資建議 2026.3.5： 
「區間操作」 ，目標價：$175

報告日期：2026.8.10 研 究 員：林秀泓

摘 要 及 調 整 原 因 
➢ CPU、記憶體缺貨比預期嚴重，導致 26H2 營收受到影響。 
➢ 無人機產品開始出貨，成為新的營收動能。

基本資料 結論與建議 
2026/8/7 收盤價： 141.50 ➢ 26Q2 營收 32.1 億元，QoQ-0.6%，YoY+5.7%，靠近財
測 QoQ-0.8%~+5.4%下緣。毛利率 47.4%(QoQ-0.2pt，
YoY-0.7pt)，下降主要因為晶圓代工成本上升影響。EPS 
2.89 元。 
➢ TouchPad方面，haptic產品已開始出貨，ASP較傳統產
品高出 5~6 倍。義隆和微軟合作開發 advance 功能，隨
不同 APP 產生不同的振動回饋，是目前市場上唯一量產
的合作夥伴，Dell、HP 正在規劃跟進。 
➢ AI 產品線以無人機應用為核心，AI BOX 於 2026/7 開始
出貨，單價 300~500 美元，遠優於傳統鏡頭模組 40~50
美元或 ISP 5~6 美元。和雷虎合作的產品已通過測試並出
貨。2026 年底前推出避開障礙功能。 
➢ 展望 26H2，因為供應商產能轉往 AI server，CPU、記憶
體缺貨比預期嚴重，導致 26H2 營收受到影響。調整預估
2026/2027 年 營 收123.4 億元 /130.2 億元，
YoY+0.1%/+5.5% ，淨利 27.1 億元 /27.8 億元，
YoY+10.9%/+2.6%，EPS 8.92 元/9.15元。 
➢ 目前股價處於 16X 26(F) PER / 15X 27(F) PER，為公司
近五年 PE 8~23X 區間中位。2026 年度配息預估 7.7 元
(2027 年配發)，推估殖利率5.4%。考量下有殖利率支撐，
但營運動能減緩，投資評等維持「區間操作」 ，下調6 個
月目標價 150 元(17X 26(F) PER  / 16X 27(F) PER)，潛
在上漲空間 6%。

資料來源：CMoney；兆豐國際預估

營 運 分 析

義隆為全球前五大 IC設計公司 
義隆成立於 1994/05，於 2001/09 上市掛牌，為台灣IC 設計公司，擁有兩大
產品類別與五大產品線，觸控類產品有觸控螢幕晶片、觸控板模組、指紋辨
識晶片等，非觸控類產品則包括指向裝置、MCU 等，26Q2 營收占比分別為
TouchPad 38%、TouchScreen 21%、指紋辨識 11%、指向裝置 15%、MCU 
3%、AI 12%。在筆電觸控領域，義隆的產品具有指標地位，觸控板、螢幕
觸控 IC、指向裝置全球市佔第一，指紋辨識晶片全球市佔第二。

圖一、義隆 26Q2 產品應用比重

資料來源：義隆；兆豐國際彙整

26Q2營收符合財測，靠近下緣 
26Q2 營收 32.1億元，QoQ-0.6%，YoY+5.7%，靠近財測 QoQ-0.8%~+5.4%
的下緣。毛利率 47.4%(QoQ-0.2pt，YoY-0.7pt)，毛利率下降主要因為晶圓
代工成本上升影響。營業利益率下降到 21.7%(QoQ-2.4pt，YoY-2.8pt)。業
外方面，主要來自金融資產評價損益佔 85%，其餘15 來自利息收入和其他，

26Q3 未提供財測  
根據規定，近 4 季業外收支佔稅前損益>10%，不提供具體財測。以營收比
重而言，AI 營收佔比上升，Touch 比例和 26Q2 持平，Non-touch 比重下滑
1~2%。毛利率因為晶圓廠 26Q3 繼續漲價，加上材料成本上升，預期26Q3
毛利率下降。因應成本增加，包括記憶體、CPU、被動元件漲價，以及晶圓
代工成本上升，公司於 2026/7/1 起調漲產品價格，漲幅 5~10%。 
參考 2026/07 月營收 我們估計 26Q3 營收 8.8 億元， MoM-17.1%，
YoY-13.2%，我們預期 26Q3 營收季減。預估 26Q3 營收 29.6 億元，
QoQ-7.7%，YoY-10.7%，毛利率47.2%，營業利益5.91 億元，QoQ-15.2%，

- 5 - 
個股報告 
2026/7 開始出貨，單價 300~500 美元，遠優於傳統鏡頭模組 40~50 美元或
ISP 5~6 美元。和雷虎合作的產品已通過測試並出貨。2026 年底前推出避開
障礙功能，4 鏡頭方案約 500 美元，多鏡頭方案 1000 美元以上。

缺料導致營運動能減緩 
展望 26H2，因為供應商產能轉往 AI server，CPU、記憶體缺貨比預期嚴重，
導致 26H2 營收受到影響。調整預估 2026/2027年營收 123.4億元/130.2 億
元，YoY+0.1%/+5.5%，淨利 27.1 億元/27.8億元，YoY+10.9%/+2.6%，EPS 
8.92 元/9.15 元。

圖四、2026 有望重回成長 
義隆各年度營收及獲利走勢預估(百萬元)

資料來源：義隆；兆豐國際預估

評價中位，下有殖利率支撐 
義隆目前股價處於 16X 26(F) PER / 15X 27(F) PER，為公司近五年 PE 
8~23X 區間中位。殖利率方面，義隆指出未來配發率 8 成以上，2026 年度
配息預估 7.7 元(2027年配發)，推估殖利率 5.4%。考量下有殖利率支撐，但
營運動能減緩，投資評等維持「區間操作」 ，下調 6 個月目標價 150 元(17X 
26(F) PER / 16X 27(F) PER)，潛在上漲空間 6%。

Yageo
Valuation
We set our target price at NT$1,450, based on 35x average 2027E-28E earnings. We value Yageo based on PE as we believe the
company’s earnings performance and industry dynamics would be the key focuses of the market. Our target multiple of 35x is
at the higher end of the trading range since 2022. During this period the company has gone through the cycles, successfully
integrated the acquired business to enhance its product offerings, and started to deliver stable margins and earnings.

Risks
Key downside risks to our target price and rating include: 1) slower-than-expected demand recovery; 2) intensified peer and
pricing competition; 3) execution risks associated with M&A integration and synergies; and 4) FX and interest rate fluctuations.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.

### Page 1

Taiwan Industrials

What’s new: We provide updates after 2Q26 results on 10 August.

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results a mixed bag. Voltronic’s 2Q26 earnings 
came in at TWD712m (EPS: TWD8.12), 1% higher than our estimate 
(TWD706m). Revenue reached TWD5.54bn (+32% QoQ; -3% YoY), 5% 
higher than our expectation as both on-line and off-line UPS demand 
recovered (on-line: grew +29/19% QoQ/YoY; off-line: grew 24% /28% 
QoQ/YoY) after demand was impacted in 1Q26 by the Middle East war and 
Voltronic’s price hikes. That said, apart from metal cost hike, Voltronic also 
suffered from the rising cost of power semi (c.20% of raw material) and 
PCB (4% of raw material) from 2Q26. Along with the existing price 
competition from inverter business (revenue was down 44% YoY in 2Q26), 
gross margin declined 0.8pp. That said, operating margin remained 18.1% 
given the lower operating expense; we believe the key reason was the 
employee stock option plan having lower cost than we expected. Lastly, 
higher FX losses of TWD210m, mainly due to CNY appreciation vs. TWD 
(up 0.9% in 2Q26) dragged net profit despite being offset by a lower tax 
rate (11.6%) due to Chinese government subsidies. 
Business update. In general, we sense that UPS demand has recovered 
from 2Q26. However, despite the inverter segment being in the peak 
season, (+60% QoQ in 2Q26), Voltronic still delivered c.44% YoY decline 
despite a low base. We attribute the weakness to the Middle East war and 
weaker-than-expected demand from South Africa. On top of this, inverter 
margins continuously declined in 2Q26 given fierce competition. We 
believe this might be the key drag to Voltronic now. In terms of gross 
margin trend, we expect Voltronic has already raised its pricing a second 
time in June to pass through the power semi and PCB cost to end 
customers. As such, we forecast gross margin will recover QoQ in 3Q26. 
However, management also indicates that they don’t rule out another round 
of price hikes due to increasing raw material cost. Lastly and most 
importantly, we learned that Voltronic has just gotten certification from a key 
HVDC client and this client has recently started a new HVDC project within 
Voltronic. As such, we expect the progress to be largely on track with our 
expectation to have more meaningful revenue contribution from 2028.

What we recommend: We slightly trim 2027-28E EPS by 1-3% on weaker 
inverter segment performance. We reaffirm our Buy (1) call and maintain 
our 12M TP of TWD1,180, with an unchanged PER of 30x applied to our 1-
year forward EPS (lower than the mid-point of its past-3-year range of 30-
46x). Key downside risk: worse-than-expected global economic growth.

How we differ: We believe we are among the few QFII brokers to 
continuously provide updates on Voltronic.

10 August 2026

2Q26 results: HVDC development largely on track

➢ A mixed bag in core business but stabilised already  
➢ HVDC development on track and will contribute in 2028 
➢ Reaffirming our Buy (1) rating; reiterating 12M TP of TWD1,180

Company profile 
Established in May 2008 and listed in March 2014, Voltronic Power Technology (Voltronic) focuses on 
the ODM business for uninterruptible power supply (UPS) related products, including off-line UPS for 
PC applications and on-line UPS for industrial applications. Its management team is mostly from 
Phoenixtec, formerly the largest UPS maker in Taiwan but was acquired by Eaton, a US-based UPS 
vendor in 2007.

Morgan Stanley Research 3
2Q Previews
Lenovo: We are 12% above the Street on adjusted net income
(non-HKFRS)
We forecast a strong start to F2027e, with 1Q27 adjusted net income of US$788mn, up
102% y/y and 12% above consensus, driven primarily by stronger margins rather than
materially higher revenue. On revenue, our forecast of US$24bn is 7% above consensus.
We expect gross margin to expand to 16.6% (+190bp y/y), reflecting continued pricing

adjustments and Lenovo's ability to pass through higher component costs despite
elevated memory prices.
However, we expect Lenovo to record a substantial non-cash charge related to "fair value
loss on derivative financial liabilities relating to warrants," potentially resulting in a
reported net loss (HKFRS). We encourage looking at net income adjusted for non-cash
items, which is US$788mn (+41% q/q, +102% y/y) on our estimates – 12% above the
Street, driven by the higher ISG revenue and margin.

For more details on our Lenovo upgrade and C2Q/F1Q preview, please see  Lenovo: From
Memory Headwind to Structural Tailwind (9 Jul 2026) and Lenovo: 1QF27/2Q26 Preview –
Increasing Conviction in ISG (6 Aug 2026)
Exhibit 1: We are 7% above consensus on revenue and 12% above on adjusted net
profit (non-HKFRS)
Source: Visible Alpha, Morgan Stanley Research estimates

### Page 4

Idea
4
Giga-Byte: We are 15% above the Street on EPS
We expect Giga-Byte's 2Q26 results to outperform market expectations, driven by
stronger AI server demand and decent motherboard and graphics card shipments.
Preliminary revenue reached ~NT$144.2bn (+37% q/q and 41% y/y), beating consensus by
13%. This strength is expected to flow through to profitability. We forecast gross margin
at 10.3%, in line with consensus, while operating margin is projected to improve to 6.1%,

60bp ahead of expectations, reflecting favorable operating leverage, with operating
income of NT$8.8bn (+19% q/q, +66% y/y) exceeding consensus by 26%. We forecast net
income of NT$5.9bn and EPS of NT$8.74, 15% above consensus estimates.
Overall, the preview points to another strong quarter of earnings outperformance,
supported by healthy demand and improving profitability.
Exhibit 2: Giga-Byte: our 2Q estimates vs. consensus –  we are 15% above the Street

on 2Q EPS
Source: Visible Alpha, Morgan Stanley Research estimates

Morgan Stanley Research 5
Asustek: We are 34% above the Street on EPS
We expect a significant 2Q26 beat, driven by robust AI server momentum and resilient PC
demand and profitability. Preliminary revenue came in at NT$241.1bn (+24% q/q and 39%
y/y), 7% above consensus. The stronger top line, together with operating leverage, is
expected to lift operating income to NT$14.2bn (+35% q/q, +138% y/y), exceeding Street
estimates by 35%. We estimate pretax income at NT$18.1bn, 36% above consensus, with

net income of NT$14.2bn and EPS of NT$19.15, 34% above consensus estimates. Although
we project gross margin to edge down sequentially to 12.7% because of product mix,
operating margin is expected to improve to 5.9%, 120bp above consensus, reflecting
better cost absorption, operating leverage and expense control.
Overall, our preview suggests another quarter of meaningful earnings outperformance,
underpinned by strong revenue growth and expanding operating profitability.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (based
on a beta of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price

Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:
We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint
of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are

rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger global NB demand
n Stronger AI server demand
n Stronger ROG Ally demand
n Better margin recovery
n Slower rise of memory prices
Risks to Downside
n Weaker global NB demand
n Weaker AI server demand
n Weaker ROG Ally demand
n Slower margin recovery
n Memory prices rises faster than expected

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Key Data  _____________________________________

GS Forecast  __________________________________

GS Factor Profile  ______________________________

2Q26 margin beat on better product mix 
All Ring reported its 2Q26 preliminary results (see Exhibit 1) on 10 
Aug. 2Q26 results beat both GSe and Bloomberg consensus due to 
higher GM, lower opex and higher non-op gains. GM in 2Q26 
reached 54.8% (vs. 51.8% in 1Q26) and came in above GSe/cons. of 
54.0%/52.5%, driven by higher mix from higher-margin 
CoWoS-related business. Meanwhile, 2Q26 OpM of 33.3% beat both 
GSe/cons. of 29.2%/29.6% thanks to better cost control and 
stronger operating leverage. Below the operating line, 
non-operating income reached NT$174mn in 2Q26, primarily 
attributable to much higher gains from equity investment, which led 
to a strong EPS (NT$8.50) beat by 36-39% vs GSe/cons. 
Strong momentum into 3Q26 but 4Q26 to slow on seasonality 
For 3Q26, management expects revenue to show strong momentum 
into 3Q26 driven by continuous CoWoS-related demand pull in. On 
the margin front, management expects GM to remain broadly in line 
with 2Q26 levels as product mix is likely similar to 2Q26. However, 
opex is now also guided to grow sequentially in 3Q26 as 
management expects higher employee bonus expenses following 
growth in pre-tax earnings. Looking further into 4Q26, management 
expects revenue to be slightly above the 2Q26 revenue level, while 
GM could see modest pressure from the ramp-up of new products, 
including panel-level packaging and CPO-related equipment. 
Overall, we now model 3Q26E revenue to grow by 29% QoQ but 
down 22% QoQ in 4Q26E, with GM of 54.9%/52.6% in 
3Q26E/4Q26E. 
Long-term growth outlook remains intact 
Looking ahead, we remain positive on All Ring’s long-term growth 
prospects thanks to ongoing technology migration as well as new 
product line ramp. We are now expecting its 2027E revenue to grow

by 44% YoY underpinned by 1) continuous ramp in CoWoS capacity, 2) gradual ramp-up 
in CPO, which is the whole new product line for All Ring, 3) higher content value in 
next-generation advanced packaging, including panel-level packaging, with new 
customer penetration, and 4) potentially new business opportunity into US customers.  
To note, despite our previous downward revision to its CPO assumptions, we continue to 
view CPO as an important next phase of long term driver for All Ring and now model 
CPO revenue to reach NT$1,861mn/NT$9,726mn in 2027E/2028E, accounting for 
14%/58% of total revenue. In advanced packaging, we expect All Ring to benefit from 
higher ASPs in panel-level packaging and see potential for the company to expand into 
the front-end portion of the panel-level packaging process. Overall, we now forecast All 
Ring’s revenue to grow 71%/44%/26% YoY over 2026-28E, with GM improving to 
53.8%/54.2%/54.9%.

CoWoS into 2027E; reiterate Buy (9 July 2026)

Earnings changes, valuation and risks 
Forecast changes 
We revise our 2026-2028E EPS by -0.8%/-6.5%/-11.4% mainly as we factor in higher 
operating expenses following management’s updated guidance on opex.

### Page 4

Maintain Buy with 12m TP revised to NT$1,450 from NT$1,550 
Alongside our earnings revisions, we revise our 12-month TP to NT$1,450 (from 
NT$1,550 previously). Our 12-month TP is based on a target P/E multiple of 35x 
(unchanged; derived by applying the implied earnings growth uplift (per GSe) to the 
company’s historical valuation framework) applied to our FY27E (unchanged) EPS. We 
view CPO as an even stronger structural tailwind for All Ring than CoWoS, with the 
potential to drive multiple-fold earnings expansion over the next several years, further 
supporting both earnings visibility and scope for valuation re-rating. Our new 12m TP 
implies 33% upside. We maintain our Buy rating on All Ring.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis - All Ring (6187.TWO) 
All Ring is one of the key suppliers of semiconductor equipment in Taiwan, primarily 
utilized in back-end advanced packaging processes. The company manufactures 
underfill dispenser, TIM heat sink attach equipment, and automated optical inspection 
equipment which are widely adopted within the CoWoS process. All Ring currently has 
nearly 100% of market share in WoS underfill dispenser and TIM heatsink attach 
equipment, and its main customers include leading foundries and OSATs in Taiwan. 
We like All Ring as we expect its revenue/earnings CAGR to reach 46%/51% in 
2025-28E, driven by 1) continued advanced packaging capacity expansion, and 2) rapid 
growth of its CPO business. With All Ring’s unique position in CPO coupling equipment 
and new packaging opportunity such as PLP , we see upside potential in its revenue and 
earnings growth and have a Buy rating on the name. 
Price Target Risks and Methodology - All Ring (6187.TWO) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on All Ring. Our 12m TP of NT$1,450 is based on a target 
P/E multiple of 35x (derived from growth-adjusted historical valuation) applied to our 
FY27E EPS. 
Key risks to our views: 1) slower advanced packaging expansion, 2) delay in adoption of 
new packaging technologies, and 3) intensifying competition.

### Page 1

Nien Made (8464.TW)
2Q26 OPM Miss on Freight Costs; Sales Acceleration and Margin 
Recovery Intact
CITI'S TAKE 
Nien Made’s 2Q26 earnings missed expectation, mainly due to elevated 
freight expenses and a higher effective tax rate, while its record GPM and 
improving July-26 sales momentum remained encouraging. We view soft 
OPM as transitory as freight costs should ease alongside lower oil prices, 
while accelerating sales growth should provide better op leverage in 2H26E. 
We lower our 26-28E EPS by 5-9% mainly reflecting higher freight expenses 
and opex assumptions and slightly trim our TP to NT$450 (18x NTM P/E). 
We remain positive on Nien Made, supported by its record GPM, 
strengthening sales trajectory and intact 2H margin recovery.      
2Q26 record GPM but high freight costs weigh on OPM — NM reported 2Q26 sales 
of NT$7.9bn, up 3% y/y but 4% below our forecast. EU was the key growth driver, 
with sales rising >16% y/y led by strong momentum in Belgium and Denmark, while 
the US remained soft amid macro uncertainty. GPM reached a record 60.0% up 70 
bps y/y, supported by mix upgrade, and pricing increases on custom products. 
However, OPM declined 380bps y/y to 27.2% owing to higher freight expenses (we 
estimate c.200bps negative impact). Net profit rose 21% y/y to NT$1.4bn, missing 
our/BBG forecast by 29%/23%, also reflecting a higher effective tax rate. 
2H26E sales growth set to accelerate — July-26 sales of NT$3.0bn increased 16% 
y/y, supported by strong growth in both US and EU, pointing to a visible acceleration 
entering 2H26E. Mgmt expects US sales to grow at a MSD y/y for FY26. Given that 
the US sales declined y/y in 1H26, this guidance implies a meaningful rebound 
during 2H26. Europe should remain another growth engine, supported by continued 
strength in Belgium and Denmark. For international markets outside NA, mgmt. 
expects HSD y/y growth with Japan and Australia also offering incremental 
momentum. 
Growth incentive program to reinforce retail partnership — Starting in July-26, 
NM introduced a growth incentive program under which part of the tariff burden will 
be refunded to their retail partners, helping strengthen long-term customer 
relationship and reinforce NM’s channel position. Separately, the company expects 
to receive tariff rebound of c.NT$300mn from the US government, which should 
help boost EPS in 2H26E. (continued)

### Page 5

Nien Made (8464.TW)
12 August 2026 Citi Research
5
Nien Made
Company description
Headquartered in Taichung, Taiwan, Nien Made is a leading global window-
covering provider, selling various blinds, shutters, shades and curtains in the
US, Europe and other countries. Nien Made provides both custom-made
window products (71% of sales in 2025) under its own brand “Norman” and
“VENETA” and ready-made products (21% of sales in 2025), selling to big-box
retailers.

Investment strategy
We rate Nien Made shares as Buy. Our investment thesis is based on: 1) the
company's ongoing share gains in custom made market in the US thanks to a
more favorable competition landscape with major peer facing financial issue.
2) The price hike of custom made products this year is able to mitigate the
tariff impact and potentially drive further margin/earnings upsides. 3)
Valuation looks attractive to us as the share is trading at a discount to its

historical average while core business remains solid.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: AVC announced its 2Q26 results and hosted an investor 
conference on 12 August 2026 after market hours. We believe AVC should 
be one of the key beneficiaries in terms of a growing TAM for thermal 
solutions with an aggressive capacity expansion plan over 2026-28. We 
reaffirm our Buy (1) call (see our latest memo, 2Q26 revenue roughly in 
line; bright 2H26 and 2027 outlook, on 7 July 2026).

What’s the impact: 2Q26 results beat. AVC’s 2Q26 revenue of 
TWD49,121m (+0.2 QoQ and +66.0% YoY) was roughly in line with our and 
Bloomberg consensus estimates. Its 2Q26 gross margin came in at 32.6% 
(vs. 29.8% in 1Q26 and 24.4% in 2Q25), a record-high level, higher than 
our and the consensus estimates of 31.0% on a better product mix and 
rising server revenue contribution of 66% in 2Q26 (vs. 66% in 1Q26 and 
51% in 2Q25). Its operating profit of TWD13,478m (+12.1% QoQ and 
+164.5% YoY) beat our and consensus estimates by 6.7% and 6.6%, 
respectively, thanks to better-than-expected operating efficiency. Thus, its 
operating margin of 27.4% (vs. 24.5% in 1Q26 and 17.2% in 2Q25), also a 
record-high level, beat our estimate (25.5%) and consensus (25.2%). With 
some non-op gains such as interest and FX and a lower-than-expected tax 
rate, its net profit of TWD9,567m (+20.9% QoQ and +139.3% YoY) beat our 
and consensus estimates by 11.9% and 10.5%, respectively. Its 2Q26 EPS 
finished at TWD24.37.

Key highlights from the investor conference – AI server revenue 
continue to rise. AVC is confident of maintaining its leading position as an 
integrator of thermal and mechanism solution and aims to be a primary 
supplier to ASICs and GPU clients. In 2027, the revenue from ASICs 
projects (with rising liquid cooling penetration, ie. 50%+ in 2027) should be 
higher than from GPU ones. AVC is working on LPU, CPO (multiple clients) 
and Rubin Ultra and aims for higher capex in 2027 (mainly in Vietnam), up 
from TWD15bn in 2026. Its 2H26 outlook should be better than that for 
1H26. We raised our 2026-28E EPS by 11-17%, mainly on our more 
aggressive revenue, gross margin and operating margin assumptions.

What we recommend: We reiterate our Buy (1) call and raise our 12M TP 
to TWD3,880 (from TWD3,155), still based on a target PER of 29x vs. a 
past-3-year PER range of 5-26x [average PER of 15x]) applied to our 1-
year forward EPS. Downside risks: slower-than-expected progress in high-
gross-margin orders, slow AI server adoption, and competition.

How we differ: Our 2026E and 2028E EPS are 7%/12% above consensus, 
likely as we are relatively more aggressive in our gross margin and 
operating margin assumptions.

Company profile 
AVC is a global leading comprehensive thermal solutions provider in segments related to desktops, 
laptops, communication, servers, energy and autos. Its main product line includes thermal products 
(thermal modules and fans), chassis, system assembly and peripheral products and hinges. The 
company has been listed on Taiwan’s main board since September 2002.

### Page 1

Summary: Merida posted its 2Q26 results on 12 August after market hours. Its operating 
profit of TWD765m (+175.7% QoQ, +8.4% YoY) beat our and the Bloomberg consensus 
estimates by 60.1% and 44.7%, respectively. However, its 2Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower 
than our forecast (TWD1.50) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.69); we attribute this to a 
net loss from its equity investments. Merida plans to hold an investor call on 14 August and 
we will provide an update then.

We have a Buy (1) rating. For more information on this company, please refer to our latest 
memo, 2Q26 revenue beat our and the consensus forecasts, on 10 July 2026.

What’s the impact 
• 2Q26 results review: core business beat but missed on equity investments. 
Merida’s 2Q26 revenue of TWD6,939m (+30.4% QoQ, -0.9% YoY) beat our and the 
Bloomberg consensus estimates by 10.3% and 2.6%, respectively. Its gross margin 
came in at 19.8% (vs. 15.4% in 1Q26; 18.8% in 2Q25), higher than our (16.5%) and the 
consensus (16.4%) estimates, which we attributed to the momentum brought by new 
models and the recovery in Europe with less price discounting and China. Thus, its 
operating margin came in at 11.0% (vs. 5.2% in 1Q26; 10.1% in 2Q25), beating our 
estimate of 7.6% and the consensus estimate of 7.8%. Its operating profit of TWD765m 
(+175.7% QoQ, +8.4% YoY) also beat our and the consensus estimates by 60.1% and 
44.7%, respectively. However, considering the equity investment loss of TWD200m+, its 
net profit of TWD318m (-2.3% QoQ, -19.2% YoY) missed our and the consensus 
estimates by 29.3% and 36.1%. Its 2Q26 EPS was TWD1.06, lower than our forecast 
(TWD1.50) and the consensus estimate (TWD1.69).

Merida: 2Q26 results vs. Daiwa and the Bloomberg estimates 
(TWDm)  Merida 2Q26 results   Daiwa forecast  Difference  Bloomberg consensus Difference 
Revenue                             6,939               6,289  10.3%        6,761  2.6% 
Gross profit                             1,374               1,038  32.4%        1,112  23.6% 
Operating profit                                765                 478  60.1%           529  44.7% 
Profit before tax                                462  591 -21.8%           671  -31.1% 
Net profit                                318                 450  -29.3%           498  -36.1%

Net margin 4.6% 7.2% -2.6pp 7.4% -2.8pp 
Source: Company, Bloomberg, Daiwa forecasts

• 2026 outlook: revenue to decline YoY while profitability improves — the worst is 
over. By region, Merida sees the demand recovery in Europe and China but some 
uncertainty in the US market. In 2H26, Merida expects the demand recovery to be 
continuous with better product mix to support its profitability.

What we recommend 
We have a Buy (1) rating. The stock is currently trading at PERs of 19.8x/16.1x, based on 
our 2026/27E EPS, vs. its past-5-year one-year-forward PER trading range of 7-33x.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Lenovo (0992.HK)
Base case, residual income model. Our key assumptions include a cost of equity of 9.3% (based
on a beta of 1.1, risk-free rate of 2.0%), a medium-term growth rate of 5.0%, and a terminal
growth rate of 3.0%.
Risks to Upside
n Faster-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Stronger PC demand recovery in China
n Better cost savings and improved product mix

n Lower-than-expected increase in memory price
Risks to Downside
n Slower-than-expected smartphone and data center turnaround
n Weaker PC demand recovery in China
n Deterioration in cost savings and product mix
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory price
Acer Inc. (2353.TW)
Base case, residual income model. We use RI model for Acer's valuation. Key assumptions
include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of 0.9, equity risk premium of 10.2% and risk-free rate

of 0.5%), a 6.0% medium-term growth rate, and a 3% terminal growth rate.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macro recovery than expected
n Share gains in the PC market
n Better-than-expected cost controls
n Lower-than-expected increase in memory cost
Risks to Downside
n Weaker macro recovery than expected
n Share loss in the PC market
n Worse-than-expected cost controls
n Higher-than-expected increase in memory cost
Asustek Computer Inc. (2357.TW)
Base case, sum of the parts:

We apply a 9x multiple to Asustek's 2026 brand profits. This is somewhat below the midpoint
of its historical band of 7-15x since 2009, which we view as fair because memory prices are
rising and will have a negative impact on margins.
We value its stakes in Pegatron and Advantech based on our price targets in proportion to
Asustek's holdings.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

Earnings revision: We factor in the 2Q26 earnings and revise up 2026E earnings by 1% 
on higher-than-expected GM and lower-than-expected Opex ratio, while non-OP is 
lower than expected. We keep 2027/ 28E earnings largely unchanged.

Valuation: Our 12m TP for Hon Hai is unchanged at NT$400 and remains based on a 
P/E. Our target 2027E P/E multiple of 18.9x is derived from a peers’ average ratio of 
trading P/E to forward year NI YoY growth and OPM (P/E divided by the sum of NI YoY 
and OPM). Our target P/E of 18.9x is in the range of company’s historical forward P/E 
since 2010, reflecting our positive view on Hon Hai’s business expansion from the 
competitive consumer electronics market to AI servers and EVs outsourcing, which 
should drive profit expansion with diversifying business opportunities for Hon Hai. 
Maintain Buy (on CL).

Price Target Risks and Methodology – Hon Hai 
Valuation methodology: We are Buy rated on Hon Hai. Our 12-month target price of 
NT$400 is based on a 18.9x 2027E P/E multiple, which is set in line with peers’ PEG&M 
ratio (i.e., P/E vs. forward-year earnings growth and OPM). 
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected ramp-up of the AI server business; (2) 
weaker-than-expected EV total solution performance across EV assembly, design, 
software, and semis; (3) slower-than-expected ramp-up of capacity globally; and (4) 
fiercer-than-expected competition in the consumer electronics EMS business.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

2Q26 beat on better opex control 
MPI reported its 2Q26 preliminary results (see Exhibit 1) on 12 Aug. 
2Q26 GM of 58.4% (59.4% 1Q26) came in slightly higher vs. GSe of 
57.9%, but inline with cons. of 58.5%. Meanwhile, 2Q26 OpM of 
34.4% exceeded both GSe/cons. of 32.1%/32.9%, driven by better 
cost control and stronger operating leverage. Overall, 2Q26 EPS of 
NT$15.55 came in above GSe and cons. by 7-10%. To note, the 
company will host an in-person analyst meeting on 14 Aug at 2pm 
Taiwan time. 
Set for GM improvement into 3Q26 and 4Q26 
Looking into 2H26, management now guides for 3Q26 revenue 
growth of 0-5% QoQ, followed by a 5-10% QoQ increase in 4Q26. 
We believe the sequential growth into 4Q26 should still be largely 
driven by the continued AI/HPC demand across both VPC and 
MEMS. To note, we expect MPI’s new US CPU customer could start 
to ramp in 4Q26. On the margin front, management expects GM to 
recover toward 1Q26 levels in 3Q26, driven by a more favorable 
product mix and price hikes (which happened in 2Q26, with positive 
effect contributing starting 3Q26), and 4Q26 GM is also now guided 
to be flat QoQ, but does not exclude the possibility for potential 
upside from new customer ramp and higher utilization rates. 
Meanwhile, management maintained its view that the 2026 opex 
ratio should remain within 25-27%, while expecting further 
operating leverage in 2027 despite continued investments in R&D 
and labor with capacity expansion. Overall, we now model 
3Q26E/4Q26E revenue growth of 10.7%/14.3% QoQ, with GM of 
59.8%/60.4%. 
Multiple growth drivers into 2027 
We expect MPI to deliver a strong revenue growth outlook with

continuous margin expansion into 2027E driven by 1) strong growth outlook from AI 
ASIC/networking customers with continuous TAM expansion, 2) significant market 
share gain within new US CPU customer, with the new customer potentially 
becoming the largest single customer in 2027, 3) the ramp of in-house PCB 
production in 3Q27, which could help lower its outsourcing ratio to 50% and help 
improve its profitability, 4) potential further price hike, and 5) emerging CPO 
opportunities. We now expect its revenue to grow by 105% YoY (vs. 61% YoY in 
2026E) with GM improving to 62.2% (vs. 59.6% in 2026E) in 2027E. 
CPO progressing well but qualification takes time 
In terms of the progress on CPO, MPI’s equipment for Insertion 3 are expected to begin 
engineering-sample shipments by around 10 units in 4Q26 to multiple customers 
spanning OSAT, foundry, and fabless clients. As for Insertion 2, MPI has completed 
on-site qualification and is now shipping to clients’ overseas production sites to do 
qualification. Overall, we remain optimistic that CPO will become the standard for 
next-generation AI networking, and we now model MPI’s CPO-related business to 
potentially contribute mid single digit % and high single digit % of its total revenue 
in 2027E/2028E (vs. high single digit % and low teens % previously).

### Page 4

Earnings changes, valuation and risks 
Forecast changes 
We revise down our 2026E EPS by -1.5% as to factor in management’s updated revenue 
growth guidance for 2H26, which came in lower vs. our prior forecasts. For 
2027E-2028E, we revise up EPS by 4.1%/6.2% mainly as to reflect our view of the 
stronger new US customer contribution, and better operating leverage as revenue 
further scales up.

Maintain Buy with TP raised to NT$8,500 from NT$8,000 
Following our upward earnings revisions, we raise our 12-month TP to NT$8,500 (from 
NT$8,000 previously). Our 12-month TP is based on a 40x 2028E discounted P/E 
(unchanged). We apply this to our 2028E EPS, and discount it back to 2027E at a 12.4% 
CoE to arrive at our TP of NT$8,000. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x beta 
(sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate (inline with GS house-view), and (3) a 
market risk premium of 6.25% (in line with our GS house view). This is also inline with our 
WinWay valuation methodology. Overall, our new TP implies c.29% upside from the 
current share price, and we maintain our Buy rating on MPI.

### Page 7

Investment Thesis, Price Target Risks and Methodology

Investment Thesis – MPI (6223.TWO) 
MPI is a leading Taiwan-based probe card provider specializing in CPC (Cantilever Probe 
Card), VPC (Vertical Probe Card), and MEMS (Micro-Electro-Mechanical Systems) Probe 
Card, which are used in the chip probe (CP) stage. The company is the 4th largest probe 
card supplier with c.7% market share (2024), and 57% of its total revenue comes from 
the probe card segment. 
We like MPI as we expect its revenue/earnings CAGR to further accelerate to 69%/95% 
in 2025-28E, driven by 1) continued share gain in vertical probe card (VPC) market via AI 
ASIC, and 2) potential share gain in MEMS probe card market through penetration into 
new both AI and non-AI customers, along with AI ASIC upgrade. MPI is also aiming to 
increase its PCB self-sufficiency to enable shorter product delivery lead time, which 
would provide it with more advantages over peers. The company is now trading above 
its average AI cycle forward P/E; however, with increasing ASP thanks to rising chip 
complexity and shorter product cadence driving frequent probe card upgrade demand, 
we continue to see upside potential to its revenue and earnings growth. We have a Buy 
rating on the name.  
Price Target Risks and Methodology – MPI (6223.TWO) 
Valuation: We are Buy rated on MPI. Our 12-month TP of NT$8,500 is based on a target 
P/E of 40x (1.7x of 3-year average forward P/E) applied to our 2028E EPS, and 
discounted back to 2027E at 12.4% CoE. Our key assumptions for CoE include: (1) 1.3x 
beta (sourcing Bloomberg), (2) a 4.25% risk-free rate, and (3) a market risk premium at 
6.25% (in line with GS house view). 
Key risks to our views: 1) softer AI/HPC demand, 2) slower penetration into new TAM, 
and 3) intensifying competition.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.
Not Rated (NR). The investment rating, target price and earnings estimates (where relevant) are removed pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when
Goldman Sachs is acting in an advisory capacity in a merger or in a strategic transaction involving this company, when there are legal, regulatory or
policy constraints due to Goldman Sachs’ involvement in a transaction, and in certain other circumstances. Early-Stage Biotech (ES). An investment

rating and a target price are not assigned pursuant to Goldman Sachs policy when this company has neither a drug, treatment or medical device that
has passed a Phase II clinical trial nor a license to distribute a post-Phase II drug, treatment or medical device.  Rating Suspended (RS). Goldman Sachs
Research has suspended the investment rating and price target for this stock, because there is not a sufficient fundamental basis for determining an
investment rating or target price. The previous investment rating and target price, if any, are no longer in effect for this stock and should not be relied

upon.  Coverage Suspended (CS). Goldman Sachs has suspended coverage of this company.  Not Covered (NC). Goldman Sachs does not cover this
company.

### Page 1

Alchip

Solid Trn3 ramp at improving production 
margins; Trn4 chipset tape out on track

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 6,100 TWD | Price: 4,210 TWD

Resilient 2Q/2H26 GMs even with strong Trainium3 ramp 
2Q26 sales was +83% QoQ with production business outgrowing NRE driven by initial 
contribution of Trainium3 from June along with healthy China ADAS SoC demand. With 
mix skewed to lower margins production, headcount expansion, annual salary hike and 
more EDA usage, 2Q26 GM/OpM was 35%/22% (vs. 50%/33% in 1Q26), taking 2Q26 
EPS to NT$20.02. While its production business could reach 80-90% of its sales in 
2H26, GM/OpM is guided to stay 20-25%/15% (Trainium1 at 10-15%/10%) on its 
growing value add in back-end design and supply chain support even with COT model. 
Trn3/4 locked in; other CSP CPU/ASIC projects in progress 
We expect Trainium3 to generate US$1.3bn sales to Alchip in ’26 (60% of its total sales) 
and could ramp further in ’27 to US$2.2bn (68% of its sales) before Trainium4 on 2nm 
tapes out in 4Q26 and enters production from 4Q27 for the dollar content uplift on 
higher chiplet design complexity and larger reticle size for the package integrating more 
compute and I/O tiles, along with meaningful volume opportunity as Amazon targets to 
adopt the chipset more aggressively in house and sell Trainium-based hardware noted in 
our preview. On top of the US$4bn revenue in ’28 and US$17bn addressable potential 
(ex-HBM) through the lifecycle for Trainium4, Alchip is engaged with other hyperscalers 
potentially on N5 ARM CPUs and certain part of the ASIC projects for contribution in ‘28. 
Firmer ‘26-28 earnings visibility at compelling valuation 
On ramping Trainium3 revenue and better Trainium4 project visibility supplemented by 
better than feared margins profile for the project from 2H26-2027, we lift ‘26/’27 EPS 
from NT$132.78/NT$201.25 to NT$132.94/NT$204.17. We keep Buy rating and raise 
PO to NT$6,100 (vs. NT$6,000 prior) still on 30x/19x ‘27/28e P/E (vs. its 6-53x range). 
Our analysis shows every 100k incremental Trn3/4 chipset units could add its EPS by 
NT$5/NT$15 in ‘27/’28, putting the share 20x/13x ‘27/28e P/E attractive on the cloud 
ASIC TAM upside.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Alchip is a pure IC design service company headquartered in 
Taiwan and has strong presence in Greater China. Its focus 
is on advanced AI/HPC chip-design, largely built on leading 
edge process by the foundry. Beyond Greater China and 
computing chips, it also has customers in US/Europe/Japan, 
etc, and from networking/gaming areas.

Investment Rationale 
We have a Buy rating on Alchip, as we still like its industry 
position for leading edge chip-design, as well as its visibility 
of sustained growth into 2027 along with continuous 
ramps from key projects on mass production side.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
Alchip (ALCPF) 
Our NT$6,100 PO is based on 30x 2027E P/E, at midcycle of the company's historical 
trading range of 6-53x. In our view, this is backed by 1) operating/net margin of 
18%/17% in 2026-27E, 2) EPS CAGR of 55% during 2026-28E, and 3) ROE of 25-30% in 
2026-28E.

Upside risks: 
1) earlier and faster ramp of other non-AWS projects to enter into production stage 
2) looser restriction from the US on China, where Alchip generates better margin with 
strong demand

Downside risks: 
1) intensified competition pressure from existing and emerging peers, 
2) failure or delay to execute node migration and corresponding product roll-out for 
customers, 
3) a slowdown in foundry node migration, which allows a longer time window for peers 
to catch up in advanced chip-design, or makes AI more easily to replace the chip-design 
service provided by the employees/engineers, 
4) lack of or insufficient supply support (foundry, back-end, etc), especially when demand 
is strong from other customers for these supply chain partners.

King Slide Works Co. Ltd.  | Asia Pacific
Preliminary Profit in July
Announced
King Slide announced its preliminary profit for July, per TWSE regulation:
• Revenue was NT$6,407mn (+44% MoM/+356% YoY), 33% of our estimate at
NT$19,555mn (+81% QoQ/+348% YoY) and 40% of the consensus at NT
$15,883mn (+47% QoQ/+264% YoY) for 3Q26.
• Pretax income was NT$5,985mn (+321% YoY; pretax margin at 93.4%), 37%
of our pretax income estimate at NT$16,377mn (margin at 83.7%) and 45% of

consensus at NT$13,170mn (margin at 82.9%) for 3Q26.
• Net income was NT$4,787mn (+321% YoY; EPS NT$50.24; NPM 74.7%), 37%
of our estimated net income at NT$12,938mn (EPS NT$135.76; NPM 66.2%)
and 46% of consensus at NT$10,460mn (EPS NT$108.72; NPM 65.9%) for
3Q26.
Our view:
• We see upside to our estimates and consensus for 3Q26 earnings.
• There should be some regular interest income and one-off FX gains during
the month, but even with that, our back-of-the-envelop calculation points to

over 85% of operating margin in July vs. 82.1% in 2Q26. We believe this
should strengthen the market's conviction about the sustainability of its
margins in 2H26.
• We maintain our view that King Slide's competitiveness of the
comprehensive patent portfolio and strong execution is entrenched and
accentuated amid the fast generation migration in AI applications.
• Stay OW with PT of NT$15,000 (30x 2027 P/E)

Valuation Methodology and Risks
King Slide Works Co. Ltd. (2059.TW)
Base case, 30x 2027e P/E. We use price/earnings as our valuation methodology because we
believe it better captures King Slide's earnings growth potential. We believe our target mul-
tiple is justified by an operating profit CAGR of 77% seen for 2025-28, implying a PEG of 0.4
vs. the average of 1.2 over 2010-24, when the stock was trading at 17-18x P/E with an operating

profit CAGR of 14%.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger-than-expected AI capex
n Stronger server demand
n Faster market share gains
Risks to Downside
n Softer AI capex
n Weaker server demand
n Sluggish kitchen furniture demand
n Competition leading to slower share gains.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

本次報告：法說會

優群 (3217 TT) 
高價庫存持續去化，NB/PC 出貨不明朗

永豐觀點 
受 NB/PC 出貨衰退影響，高價庫存去化較緩慢，2Q26 毛利率持續呈壓。再次下
修 NB/PC 出貨預估，僅在 Metal Bar 、CAMM 系列及 TypeC 產品可保持較佳成
長動能，股價評價位階低，可逢低長線佈局。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進投資建議：公司再次下修 NB/PC 出貨預估，且客戶更改記憶體設計情形
短期 SO-DIMM 需求恐將下滑，相關產品線如SO-DIMM、M.2 及 U-DIMM 今明
年動能皆較不明朗。研究部下修今明年獲利預估，預估 2026/2027 年 EPS 分別為
11.06 及 12.31 元。維持買進投資建議，因獲利調整目標價由 213 元調降至 172
元(14X 2027F EPS)。

### Page 2

2

公司簡介：優群成立於 1987 年，於 2004 年掛牌上櫃，主要經營業務為電子零組
件及連接器產品之設計、製造及銷售。產品廣泛應用於電子產業，涵蓋 NB、伺服
器、桌上型電腦及手機等領域。終端客戶皆為國際知名大廠，目前主要生產據點包
括台灣新竹、中國昆山、遂寧及越南。目前越南廠一期已於4Q25 投產，而二廠部
分預計將在 2H26 規劃。2Q26 主要各產品比重分別為：SO-DIMM 28% 、M.2 
26%、TypeC 13%、Long-DIMM 11%、Metal Bar 12%、其他 10%。 
高價庫存持續去化 ， 出貨下修營收獲利不如預期 ：2Q26 營收 9.47 億元(-10% YoY，
-0.1% QoQ)，稅後獲利 2.14 億元(+2.9% YoY)，EPS 2.38 元，SO DIMM 及 M.2
產品仍是受到 NB 出貨持續下修影響分別年減 29%及 18%，其中 SO DIMM 衰退
幅度較原先預期嚴重 ；Long-DIMM 部分 ，PC 出貨亦呈現衰退 ， 年減18%；Metal 
Bar 部分，美系品牌手機需求穩健及新 NB 機種採用，成長近 47%。2Q26 單季毛
利率 47.52%，低於原先預估的 49.15%，主要因黃金等高價庫存隨出貨下修本季
去化較慢，毛利率持續受壓抑。 
再次下修 NB/PC 產品出貨，營收動能疲緩：優群今明年各產品線主要動能如下：
(1) SO-DIMM 衰退情況較預期嚴重：目前筆電出貨仍不理想，客戶端持續受限於
記憶體、CPU 缺貨影響，被迫更改設計及調度機種，導致 SO DIMM 需求將持續
下降。研究部預期產品線年衰退幅度恐擴大至20%，明年產品線展望亦尚未明朗。
(2) Long-DIMM 桌機與 Server 用呈現兩極：優群去年有兩家美系 CSP 客戶訂
單，今年預計持續加量，在 R-DIMM 部分出貨符合預期。惟桌機出貨方面，同受
記憶體缺貨影響，U-DIMM 大幅下滑，使整體產品線成長呈壓。目前R-DIMM 出
貨佔比已逾 6-7 成，R-DIMM 出貨佔比提升可改善產品線毛利率 。 下修整體Long-
Dimm 今明年呈小幅成長。(3) M.2 亦受 NB 出貨下修，產品線僅持平至小幅成
長：優群目前 M.2 產品市佔仍逾 50%，高階 NB 逐步導入 PCIe Gen5 SSD、Wifi 
7，規格升級亦使 M.2 相關產品線保有成長動能，惟亦因筆電出貨較不明朗，預期
產品線僅持平至小幅成長看待。目前 Gen5-1A 佔比約 10%，ASP 較高。NB 廠因
高階新機種筆電增加 SSD 插槽，預估 Gen5 滲透率可持續增加。(4) Metal Bar 獨
供美系品牌應用 ，今明年持續保持高成長 ：目前微型沖壓件 Metal Bar 獨家供應美
系品牌手機，應用於 SiP 模組。及新訂單美系品牌 NB 應用在 Wifi Module ，其
ASP 為倍數，預期今年產品線成長可逾 30%。而在 NB 新應用上明年新機預計導
入 Wifi 8，Metal Bar 用量將呈 3 倍提升，明年成長性亦可期。(5) TypeC 穩定成
長，客戶改變新設計 ASP 可以提升：TypeC 多為專案基礎，應用在高階機種如
Thunderbolt 5 客戶採用優群產品 ， 預期今年呈雙位數成長 。 在明年部分 ， 因TypeC
產品客戶改變設計欲使連接器可做替換，預期 ASP 將可提升 50%，預期明年產品
線亦可保持成長 。(6) CAMM/CABB 持續出貨 ， 惟亦受NB 出貨下修影響 ：LPDDR5

營運現況與分析

CAMM 2、CABB 皆已持續出貨，今年產線將擴充增速。2Q26 CABB 客戶庫存調
整影響出貨下降。而今年中系 NB 客戶高階產品導入 CAMM 666 pin ，5 月已量
產出貨產品目前月產能 50K， 仍是些微受到記憶體模組影響 。 略為下修今年營收佔
比可逾 3-5%。在明年部分，中系 NB 客戶目前 CAMM 需求穩定、美系 NB 客戶
亦可在 2Q27 量產，高階機種約占 10%，單價為 SO DIMM 10 倍，對整體產品線
明年展望亦相對樂觀。整體而言，今年 PC/NB 出貨再次下修，展望較不明朗。持
續觀察 NB 更改設計情況是否為短期影響，下一世代 DDR6 產品優群具備競爭利
基，長線成長動能較可期。 
未來營運及獲利預估：評估優群今年整體營收恐較疲軟，3Q26 高價庫存消化完畢
毛利率應可改善。預估 3Q26 營收 11.39 億元(+20% QoQ ，+1%YoY)，毛利率
48.29%，稅後獲利 2.85 億元(+33%QoQ，-25% YoY) ，EPS 3.17 元。調整預估
2026/2027 年營收分別 41.09 及 43.67 億元(-1% YoY ，+6.3% YoY) ，毛利率分
別為 48.34%及 49.67%，稅後獲利分別為 9.95 及 11.08 億元(-13%YoY，+11% 
YoY)，EPS 分別為 11.06 及 12.31 元。維持買進投資建議，下修今明年獲利，目
標價由 213 元調降至 172 元(14X 2027F EPS)。

力成 (6239 TT)  Buy

記憶體和先進封測需求增，建議 Buy，目標價 384 元。  
投資建議 ：記憶體及 AI 需求暢旺，使力成整體產能緊張，營收
有望持續上揚，公司積極精進 AI 相關先進封測技術，認證通過
後，出貨量大增，將為力成新成長動能。預估力成 2026 年稅後
EPS 12.81 元。力成受惠先進封測產品的開發與產能擴充，客戶
新產品開發及驗證持續進行中， FOPLP 等高階封裝營收貢獻將
逐步增加。力成亦有切入  CPO ，正處於產品驗證階段。之前
AMD (AMD US) 與力成合作面板級封測，代表力成技術已取得
Tier-1 客戶認可，力成 2026~2027 年台灣先進封裝產能已由
AMD 等客戶訂滿，與 Broadcom 合作後，高階封裝營收貢獻將
更明顯。因此建議 Buy ，目標價 384 元。  
2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.92 元：記憶體、 HPC 和 AI 產品測試的強勁需
求，力成 2Q26 營收持續成長，為 231.16 億元， QoQ+8.45% ，
其中來自 NAND Flash 增長較多。營收比重方面， DRAM 占總
營收比重為 19% 。 NAND Flash 增至 29% ，客戶需求維持高檔；
SiP/Module 占營收比重維持在 11%；邏輯占營收比重則微降至  
41%。毛利率上升至 21.76% ，增加 2.32 個百分點，受惠產能利
用率提升和較佳產品組合變化。力成 2Q26 稅後 EPS 2.92 元。  
預期 2026 年力成整個集團資本支出約 500 億元 ：力成 2025 年
整個集團資本支出約 195 億元，由於先進製程需求佳， 2026 年
力成整個集團資本支出將會非常顯著增加 ， 預估大幅增加至 500
億新台幣。折舊費用方面， 2025 年和 2026 年將分別為 123 億
和 135 億左右。資本支規劃方面， 300~400 億投資在力成本身，
其中 200~300 億主要投資在 FOPLP 相關，擴充的產能可支援
2026~2028 年；100~150 億則投資在超豐 /Tera Probe/TeraPower 。 
為滿足 AI 相關需求 ， 為特定美國大客戶建置全新 FOPLP 產線 。
力成 2026 年將全心投入 FOPLP 產品認證、小量生產及產能擴
充，期待 2027 年後有明顯的營收貢獻。

3 群益投顧 
需求逐步向上，力成 2Q26 營收 231.16 億元， QoQ+8.45% ，稅前淨利 36.61
億元， QoQ+15.96% ，稅後 EPS 2.92 元：  
受 AI 應用帶動，記憶體市場與 HBM 需求持續強勁，隨著 AI 應用擴展，帶
動大型晶片與先進封裝需求提升。力成 2Q26 營收為 231.16 億元，
QoQ+8.45% ，其中來自 NAND Flash 增長較多。  
力成  2Q26 各項產品需求持續向上， 2Q26 記憶體方面， DRAM 客戶市場
需求持續強勁，營收持續維持動能。 NAND Flash 部分， NAND 隨著  AI 
server 等  eSSD 需求上揚，營收續增； eSSD 需求及出貨暢旺，挹注相關營
收，季營收 QoQ/YoY 續增。 Enterprise SSD 需求明顯， QoQ/YoY 續增。  
邏輯業務， 2Q26 超豐營收 52.84 億元， QoQ+11.89% ，稅前淨利 11.09 億元，
QoQ+10.00% ，稅後 EPS 1.57 元，客戶延續 1Q26 拉貨動能，營收維持高檔。
Tera Probe / Terapower: 配合客戶計畫，持續投入 CAPEX 營收  QoQ 、 YoY
亦續增雙位數。   
力成整體產品營收比重方面， 2Q26 依產品類別來看：  DRAM 占總營收比
重微降至  19% （ 1Q26 為  20% ）；NAND Flash 占力成營收比重從  26% 上
升至  29% ，客戶需求維持高檔； SiP/Module 占營收比重維持在 11%；邏輯
占  2Q26 營收比重則微降至  41% （ 1Q26 為  43% ）。若以服務類別來看：
測試（ Testing ）占營收比重上升至  23% （ 1Q26 為  22% ）； SiP/Module 占
營收比重維持在  11% ；封裝（ Packaging ）占營收比重則為  66% （ 1Q26 為
67% ）。  
2Q26 毛利率上升至 21.76% ，較 1Q26 的 19.44% 增加 2.32 個百分點，受惠
產能利用率提升和較佳產品組合變化，力成 2Q26 封裝產能利用率為 90%，
測試為 85%。力成 2Q26 稅前淨利 36.61 億元， QoQ+15.96% ，稅後 EPS 2.92
元。  
力成整個集團 2026 年資本支出約 500 億元，著重 FOPLP 等高階封測產能
投資 ：  
力成 2025 年整個集團資本支出約為 195 億元，由於先進製程需求佳， 2026
年力成力成整個集團資本支出將會非常顯著增加，原預估 400 億新台幣，但
再增加購買友達廠，力成公告的資本支出已達 200 多億元，因此力成將 2026
年資本支出增加至約 500 億元。折舊費用方面， 2025 年和 2026 年將分別為
123 億和 135 億左右。  
資本支出規劃方面， 300~400 億投資在力成本身，其中 200~300 億主要投資
在 FOPLP 相關，擴充的產能可支援 2026~2028 年； 100~150 億則投資在超
豐 /Tera Probe/TeraPower 。  
超豐 Flip Chip 封裝產能已滿載，在  QFN 封裝的月產能大約為  1.8 億顆
（ 180M ）。在  LGA 封裝方面的月產能大約為  3,000 萬顆（ 30M ）。以目
前現有的產能設備，在產線滿載的情況下，每個月可以創造大約新台幣 2.6 
億元的營收 。 超豐在09/2026 底還會有一批全新採購的  LGA 封裝設備陸續
進駐，預計在  10/2026 完成裝機，預計 4Q26 將達到每月 230m 的產能，若
滿載營收將達到 3.2 億元 /月。超豐也已投入第三代半導體封測設備，進行
客戶產品驗證中。

4 群益投顧 
Tera Probe/TeraPower 持續投入大規模資本支出，以推動  HPC 、 AI 和
ADAS 產品的成長， 2025 年投資金額接近  50 億新台幣上下，其中 ADAS，
投入 21 億新台幣， AI 投入近 18 億新台幣， HPC 投入 2.7 億新台幣，相對
較少是因為  HPC 之前已有較大的投資。預估 2026 年的資本支出估計約在
3 億美元上下。  
晶兆成（ TeraPower ）進行大量 AI 晶片的  CP/FT 測試，為擴充產能，已收
購新廠房，預計未來 2 年可新增 400–500 台測試機台，以支援  AI 、 HPC 及
ADAS 的強勁需求。  
此外力成也有和華邦電合作，包括但不限於 Chip on Wafer 、Bumping 及 TSV 
via-reveal 封裝等， CoW 機台也到位。華邦電的矽中介層技術，是在開發
CUBE DRAM 系列的伴隨產品 ， 專攻高效能AI 邊緣運算 ， 結合力成2.5D/3D
異質整合封裝技術。  
力成 TSV 則以影像處理為主，運用之前和聯電、 Elpida(Micron) 合作的 TSV
產能和技術發展 WLCSP 用於醫療 (全球基因定序檢測龍頭 illumina) 、監控、
自動化及工業用的 CIS CSP 2024 年開始量產 (HVM) ，已經從最早消費性安
全監控跨入工業用和車用 (TOF) 。公司的良率達 95% 以上。  
P11 廠 (面板級封裝產線 )預計於 04/2026 完成無塵室擴建：總產能目標 6K 
panels/month( 約當  21K 片 12 吋晶圓 )。力成購入之  P12 廠  ( 原友達廠房 ) 
在  AI 布局中扮演解決產能瓶頸之關鍵角色。一層規劃做  middle-end 
online （即晶圓中段製程）。二層規劃做安裝用於  AI 晶片與  ABF 載板整
合 (OS, On Substrate) 的設備，預計 3Q26 安裝完成，解決後段組裝產能不
足的問題。此外， P12 廠亦是部署  5 倍光罩大 AI 晶片之基地，相關稀缺
之貼合機台將進駐該廠，使力成具備處理超大型晶片封裝之獨特能力。 P12 
廠  1 樓空間則保留作為未來  FOPLP 擴充或新技術研發之儲備空間，為
2028 年至  2029 年之長期成長預作準備。同時，為追求最佳的生產效率，
也正同步在該廠區安裝各項高階的自動化生產機台。  
AI 需求持續帶動，預估力成 3Q26 營收 246.66 億元， QoQ+6.71% ，稅前淨
利 40.97 億元， QoQ+11.90% ，稅後 EPS 3.56 元：  
3Q26 展望，受 AI 應用需求帶動，記憶體市場與 HBM 需求持續強勁，價格
與需求同步上揚。隨著  AI 基礎建設持續擴展，帶動高階與大型晶片之先
進封裝需求提升。記憶體與先進封裝業務持續為力成集團成長的主要動能，
公司將持續推進  FCBGA 、FOPLP 等關鍵技術布局以強化未來成長動能。此
外，供應鏈亦需持續關注美伊衝突帶動能源價格波動，以及美國利率政策對
半導體業之衝擊與影響。   
各產品線方面， DRAM 產品相關部份，記憶體產業目前仍處於供不應求狀
態，且客戶持續將晶圓產出極大化，有利於維持力成封測整體的產能利用
率。記憶體已成為  AI 基礎設施的關鍵要素， HBM 與標準型記憶體需求皆
穩定成長，並間接帶動封裝技術由傳統打線邁向先進封裝，預期未來對營收
與毛利皆有貢獻。車用部分，逐步高階自主化帶動記憶體與存儲需求增加，
預計將有成長空間。  
NAND Flash 方面，受惠於  AI 相關應用擴展， NAND Flash 封測訂單在資
料中心與企業級  SSD 需求持續成長帶動 ， 雖然智慧型手機需求可能受到記

憶體價格上漲的影響而下滑，但整體動能穩健。除需求面成長外，因應封裝
材料及金線成本上升，力成將適度反映於客戶價格，預期對 3Q26 營收及毛
利率都有較正面的支撐。  
邏輯晶片方面， 3Q26 雖然手機等消費性產品偏向保守，不過在高階封裝
FCBGA 需求持續成長及新產品陸續量產下，整體產能利用率仍維持高檔水
位。在高階 FCBGA 領域，力成已順利實現大尺寸  FCBGA 多晶片模組
（ MCM ）封裝的量產，並跨入 1 倍光罩尺寸（ 1× reticle size ）大晶片封裝
的量產階段。同時亦彰顯公司在大晶片製造領域於高精度貼片、散熱管理及
量產良率等方面的深厚競爭優勢。因此力成已具備承接 AI 及 HPC 應用訂
單的能力。  面對原物料成本上漲，已與客戶積極溝通將相關成本反映於產
品價格中。隨著價格逐步調整，預期將有助於支撐整體毛利率。先進封測
FOPLP 除持續深化客戶端合作及加速樣品驗證外，更同步與主要材料及設
備供應商展開密切合作，以確保整體供應鏈能穩定運作，期望如期於 2027
順利交付量產。  
3Q26 Tera Probe /TeraPower 預計營收將維持穩定成長，主要得益於受伺服
器及機器學習 /人工智慧應用強勁需求的帶動。而消費型 DRAM 和消費型邏
輯產品的需求溫和增加。在車用領域方面， TPJ 需求穩固， TPW 在先進駕駛
輔助系統 (ADAS) 相關需求亦維持穩定。  
超豐的部份， AI 與高效能運算（ HPC ）持續帶動 Bumping 及 FC 封裝需求。
智慧型手機新品上市及消費性電子旺季將帶動出貨。車用與工業市場可望
持續成長。 AI 相關需求促進 PC/ 手機 /智能家居新產品應用普及，地端裝置
將推升 Edge AI 需求，加速升級擴張。因應原物料成本持續上漲，提升單價
及產品毛利。  
毛利率方面，預估力成本身 3Q26 封裝產能利用率 85~95% ，測試 85~95% 。
加上公司自 4Q25 起，逐季調漲價格，幅度為個位數至雙位數，部份反應材
料上漲，金線等特殊材料漲價採個案談判，多數自 1Q26 生效，成本控管得
宜下，有助毛利率上揚。預估力成 3Q26 營收 246.66 億元， QoQ+6.71% ，稅
前淨利 40.97 億元， QoQ+11.90% ，稅後 EPS 3.56 元。  
力成發展 PLP 已久，公司有 5X reticle 晶片工程品，期待 2027 年 AMD 有
明顯營收貢獻：  
05/21/2026 AMD 宣布將在台灣產業體系投資超過 100 億美元，以擴大策略
合作夥伴關係，提升下一代 AI 基礎設施的先進封裝製造產能，並提出和力
成合作面板級先進封裝技術 。 AMD 與力成合作的意義在於核心 CPU(Venice)
導入力成 2.5D panel level 技術。  
力成為滿足 AI 相關需求，為特定美國大客戶 (AMD) 建置全新 FOPLP 產線。

從頭到尾  (Turnkey) 完整解決方案的公司之一。

Package size 小於  3X reticle 之製程已驗證， 5X reticle 尺寸之機台於
03/2026 進場並認證。以每片 12 吋 wafer 來看， 5X reticle 晶片僅可生產 8~9
顆，若用力成 panel solution 可以做到 45 顆 /片。 14x reticle 尺寸機台與供
應商共同開發中，預計 2026 年底設備到位。  
2025 年到 1H26 主要營收來自策略夥伴的 NRE 。力成從 RDL 製作到封裝模
組，良率已達 90% 至接近 95%，很快就可以達到客戶標準出貨。力成在 2026
年將全心投入 FOPLP 產品認證、小量生產及產能擴充，預計 2H26 將展開
客戶產品的試產與驗證（ Qualification ），目標在 2027 年中以前正式出貨，
若以 20K/ 月計算，預估貢獻月營收 10 億元台幣。  
產能方面，優先將第一階段產能 2K 片 /月的機台設備全部準備就緒，並將其
標準作業流程（ SOP ）與製造參數完全定案。目前 2K 片規模的設備皆已全
部點交完畢並安裝成功，設備到貨狀況完全正常。一般而言，先進封裝的設
備交期都相當長， PLP 所需的特定設備目前平均交期大約需要 1 年；且設
備到貨後，還需要大約半年的時間進行裝機、調試與產品認證。如果是現在
才準備要下單投入  Fan-out PLP 技術的公司，從下單到通過客戶認證並正
式量產，約需 3 年。  
目前所有支援  AMD 專案的先進封裝設備皆已全數進駐並安裝完畢，正密
集進行客戶端驗證。

矽光子封裝將在 2026 年底小量量產 ：  
力成在矽光子領域並非從事電路設計，核心優勢在於利用既有的先進封裝
技術，特別是利用微凸塊（ Micro-bump ）技術發展  Hybrid CPO （共同封裝
光學元件）。傳統的光學模組多採插拔式  (Plug-in) ，但未來的趨勢是將光
學引擎盡可能貼近  AI 晶片 。 力成利用自身在 Bumping technology 的強項 ，
開發出基於凸塊的光學引擎 (Bump-based Optical Engine) 。  
力成相關技術主要分為以下兩大服務項目：

### Page 7

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 8 月 11 日

7 群益投顧 
1)TSV 與 Bump 的 3D 光學引擎封裝：利用矽穿孔（ TSV ）與凸塊（ Bump ）
作為基礎連接的 Optical Engine 封裝技術，將 PIC 與 EIC 進行整合。此項
3D 封裝技術已完成主要客戶的工程能力驗證，預計在 2026 年底前即可進
入量產。  
2) CPO 定義即是將光學引擎與  AI 晶片封裝在一起。力成透過 FOPLP 平
台（ 510mm x 515mm 面板）提供的高密度重布線層  (RDL) 與晶片整合技
術，能夠實現這種高整合度的封裝需求。將光學引擎與 SoC、HBM（高頻寬
記憶體）或 Switch （交換器）整合封裝在一起，藉此達到最大的傳輸效能與
節省耗電。  
具體進度如下：  
2026 年底前光學引擎 (OE) 小量生產。力成在 2026 年初已利用成熟的 micro-
bump 技術開發出 Optical Engine （光學引擎） ，包含模組與 OBO/OSFP 技術
的工程樣品正陸續送交客戶端驗證，預計年底前可開始小量生產。  
2H27 CPO 交換器出貨。力成計畫將光學引擎與交換器晶片（ Switch IC ）整
合，因為交換器的架構相對簡單、驗證速度較快。  
2028 年 OE 整合 AI 晶片量產。由於超大尺寸 AI 晶片的架構更為複雜，且
其搭配的光學引擎設計仍在不斷演進，將光學引擎整合至 AI 晶片上的 CPO
技術，預計要到 2028 年或 2028 年底才會正式邁向量產出貨。  
力成與 Broadcom 於新加坡共同設立面板級先進封裝 (Panel Level 
Packaging, PLP) 製造合資公司 ：  
力成董事會決議將與 Broadcom 於新加坡共同設立面板級先進封裝 (Panel 
Level Packaging, PLP) 製造合資公司，合資公司為 XCIP TECHNOLOGIES
PTE. LTD. 投資總金額 56.6 億美元，力成出資美金 4 億元，持股比例 30%，
未來將依新加坡廠區建置及資金需求分階段投入，因此不會增加力成 2026
年原訂 500 億元新台幣的資本支出計畫，以自有資金支應。但仍須視主管機
關核准結果、相關交易文件簽署等條件完成。因本投資案與 Broadcom 有簽
署保密協議，公司將依核准內容及相關法令規定，辦理後續交易事項及資訊
揭露。  
雙方共同開發及生產可用於先進封裝基板上、實現極細線寬的重佈線層
（ RDL ）封裝技術。也就是在先進的基板（ substrate ）上，直接進行 RDL 技
術與  fine line space （細線寬間距）技術。該合資公司將專為  Broadcom 提
供服務，這並不會影響力成科技既有及未來客戶在 Fan-out PLP 領域的既
有合作。  
力成的角色主要為營運封測廠的管理和製造，將在台灣多年經營面板級封
裝實務經驗，移轉至合資廠。 Broadcom 主要扮演財務投資與保障大單，使
力成未來會有穩定的訂單，預期力成未來將採權益法認列此合資公司獲利。
新產品帶動營收成長，預估力成 2026 年營收 949.27 億元， YoY+26.69% ，
稅前淨利 153.78 億元， YoY+74.91% ，稅後 EPS 12.81 元：  
力成以往因記憶體客戶集中度高，受單一客戶的影響較大。力成在記憶體產
業耕耘以久，記憶體產業寡占的特色也不易改變，記憶體廠和封測廠的關係
